- **mneme-kimi-code**: Affiliate-Werkzeug (kein Abteilungs-OS): projektbezogenes Gedächtnis über Sessions hinweg — sieben Hooks schreiben Beobachtungen in eine lokale SQLite-Datenbank; der Skill mem-search und die MCP-Werkzeuge holen sie nach dem Prinzip der progressiven Offenlegung wieder heraus. Läuft in Claude Code und Kimi Code, alles bleibt lokal. HOST-ANFORDERUNG: `uv` (Hooks und MCP-Server laufen über `uv run`). Lizenz AGPL-3.0, externes Repo, gepinnt auf v2.0.24. ([ArchiDoxx/mneme-kimi-code](https://github.com/ArchiDoxx/mneme-kimi-code))
- **mnemex**: MCP runtime for semantic code search and AST analysis, ranking symbols by PageRank. Optional engine behind the code-search facade, and usable directly for its full tool surface. ([MadAppGang/magus](https://github.com/MadAppGang/magus/tree/HEAD/plugins/mnemex))
- **mnemic**: A memory for Claude that lasts: what you tell it is kept as dated facts in one SQLite file on your own machine and recalled in every later conversation. Facts carry when they were true, corrections keep their history, and open questions are put to you instead of guessed. No account, no service. Native binary for macOS (Apple Silicon) and Windows; no Java needed. ([thegreystone/claude-plugins](https://github.com/thegreystone/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/mnemic))
- **mnemo**: Persistent memory with session tracking, passive capture, and memory protocol. Gives Claude a brain that survives across sessions. ([jmeiracorbal/mnemo](https://github.com/jmeiracorbal/mnemo/tree/HEAD/plugin/claude-code))
- **mnemo**: Claude Code forgets your corrections. mnemo doesn't. ([xyrlan/mnemo](https://github.com/xyrlan/mnemo))
- **mnemo**: Adds memory_add/memory_search, checkpoint/resume, and named documents to Claude Code and Codex, backed by your own local Qdrant + fastembed. ([pacheco20222/mnemo](https://github.com/pacheco20222/mnemo))
- **mnemo**: Persistent memory with session tracking, compaction recovery, and Memory Protocol. Gives Claude a brain that survives across sessions. ([MiguelAguiarDEV/mnemo](https://github.com/MiguelAguiarDEV/mnemo/tree/HEAD/plugin/claude-code))
- **mnemo-mcp**: Persistent AI memory across sessions ([n24q02m/claude-plugins](https://github.com/n24q02m/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/mnemo-mcp))
- **mnemo-memory**: Persistent memory for Claude Code. Auto-loads memories on session start, auto-saves on stop, with on-demand store/recall skills. ([you06/mnemos](https://github.com/you06/mnemos/tree/HEAD/claude-plugin))
- **mnemograph**: Persistent memory for Claude Code sessions via mnemograph knowledge graph ([tm42/mnemograph](https://github.com/tm42/mnemograph/tree/HEAD/mnemograph-claude-code))
- **mnemon**: Capture articles, videos, podcasts, books — get structured, searchable knowledge artifacts framed by your personal context ([dkushnikov/mnemon-plugin](https://github.com/dkushnikov/mnemon-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/mnemon))
- **mnemonic**: Knowledge base with semantic search across 5 business domains. Progressive depth layers optimize token usage. ([marioser/mnemonic](https://github.com/marioser/mnemonic/tree/HEAD/plugin))
- **mnemonic**: Save and retrieve durable work, record caller-reported completion evidence, assess declared repository scope without semantic claims, compare drafts with possible existing work, review permanent duplicate merges, coordinate human questions, and preserve truthful audit history, retrieve durable project activity, and author concise human-facing closeout reports. Includes exact external tracker references and bounded explicit external comparison. ([RightBrain-Networks/mnemonic](https://github.com/RightBrain-Networks/mnemonic/tree/HEAD/plugin))
- **mnemonica**: Keeps a project's architectural documentation in sync with its code by having Claude maintain modular docs as it works. ([HAX-Studio/mnemonica](https://github.com/HAX-Studio/mnemonica))
- **mnemopay**: Payment infrastructure for AI agents. Agent Credit Score (300-850), real payment rails (Stripe/Paystack/Lightning), autonomous shopping with escrow, hash-chained ledger, and behavioral finance. Charge for tool usage, track spending, shop autonomously, and build agent creditworthiness all from Claude Code. ([anthropics/claude-plugins-community](https://github.com/anthropics/claude-plugins-community/tree/HEAD/claude-plugin))
- **mnemos**: Persistent memory for Claude Code — capture, digest, and recall project knowledge across sessions with a dependency-free Go CLI and hook integration. ([jeremylongshore/tons-of-skills-marketplace](https://github.com/jeremylongshore/tons-of-skills-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/community/mnemos))
- **mnemos**: The mnemos server provides a persistent memory system for AI coding agents that compounds corrections into skills via deterministic promotion. Single Go binary, MCP-native, runs locally. ([polyxmedia/mnemos](https://github.com/polyxmedia/mnemos))
- **mnemos-memory**: Biomimetic long-term memory plugin for Claude Code with MCP server integration. ([anthony-maio/mnemos](https://github.com/anthony-maio/mnemos))
- **mnemosyne**: Extract → route → dispatch session learnings CLI. Self-contained bundle — no separate npm install required. ([nwlnexus/mnemosyne](https://github.com/nwlnexus/mnemosyne))
- **mnogovid-code-scanner**: Multi-scanner security orchestration with optional AI and independent-agent triage. ([BergaBruh/mnogovid](https://github.com/BergaBruh/mnogovid/tree/HEAD/plugins/mnogovid-code-scanner))
- **mnogovid-system-scanner**: Consent-gated Linux host security assessment and bounded network observation. ([BergaBruh/mnogovid](https://github.com/BergaBruh/mnogovid/tree/HEAD/plugins/mnogovid-system-scanner))
- **mo-cmux**: Preview Markdown files in cmux browser split pane using mo ([monzou/mo-cmux](https://github.com/monzou/mo-cmux/tree/HEAD/plugins/mo-cmux))
- **mo-es-governance**: Constitutional governance framework for Claude Code. Set behavioral modes (High Security, High Integrity, Creative, Research, and more), enforce role hierarchy (Primary → Secondary → Observer), control posture (SCOUT / DEFENSE / OFFENSE), inject governance documents, and maintain cryptographic SHA-256 audit trails. Includes hooks for session-start governance restore, pre-tool enforcement, post-tool audit logging, and prompt injection. Patent-pending (Serial No. 63/877,177). Built by Ello Cello LLC. ([SunrisesIllNeverSee/moses-governance](https://github.com/SunrisesIllNeverSee/moses-governance))
- **mo-es-governance-for-cowork**: Constitutional AI governance for Claude Cowork — no shell dependencies, prompt-native enforcement. Set behavioral modes (High Security, High Integrity, Creative, Research, and more), enforce role hierarchy (Primary → Secondary → Observer), and control posture (SCOUT / DEFENSE / OFFENSE) directly in any Cowork session. Includes 8 governance skills and 8 slash commands. Built on MO§ES™ by Ello Cello LLC. Patent-pending (Serial No. 63/877,177). ([SunrisesIllNeverSee/moses-governance-cowork](https://github.com/SunrisesIllNeverSee/moses-governance-cowork))
- **moa**: Mixture-of-Agents for Claude Code: fan a task out to configurable opencode models (read-only over your repo), coordinated by a subagent, then let Claude aggregate, act, and have the experts review its plan and diff. ([jackmcpickle/claude-moa](https://github.com/jackmcpickle/claude-moa))
- **moa**: Multi-model committee: your agent chairs up to 4 heterogeneous LLM members that blind-review, decide, and brainstorm in parallel — independent generation → structured interaction → evidence-driven convergence. ([sdsrss/moa-skill](https://github.com/sdsrss/moa-skill))
- **moai-accountant**: 재무·세무 전담 AI 코워커 — 재무제표 분석·결산 관리·차이 분석·IR, 개인 세금 절약·가계 예산·투자 입문·보험 적합성 스킬 11종. OpenDART 전자공시 MCP 연동. 이런 분께 추천: 사업자·재무 담당자·개인 재테크 입문자. ※ 세무사·회계사 자문 대체가 아닌 참고 자료입니다. ([modu-ai/moai-cowork](https://github.com/modu-ai/moai-cowork/tree/HEAD/plugins/moai-accountant))
